/*--9110309302109404940810*/
/*RZ960310 For multi language*/
/*RZ970425- Add cmpReferUser*/
/*RZ14000622- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspRpt_CompanyGroupAddress]
(	
/*
	EXEC CRMspRpt_CompanyGroupAddress 3, 1033, 1065 
*/
@UserId INT,
/*RZ960310 For multi language*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(8000)*/
/*RZ960310 For multi language*/
DECLARE @txt NVARCHAR(MAX)

SET @txt = 'CrmCompany'
SELECT @txt = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE UserId = @UserId
AND TypeId = 1
/*--PRINT @txt*/ 
/*RZ960310 For multi language*/
/*In the sp , we use * because in the table CrmsysDBTempInfo All selects Are multi language */
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	Exec   
	(
		'
		SELECT 
			*,
			/*
			add   * and
			cach in 9512 for return by number of prs
			DISTINCT*/
			/*
			CompanyID,
			cmpNumber,
			cmpTitleId,
			/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = tmp.cmpTitleId) cmpTitle,
			cmpName,*/

			/*RZ14000622- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(tmp.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = tmp.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''') 
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = tmp.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) cmpTitle,
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(tmp.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN (ISNULL(tmp.[cmpName],''''))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = tmp.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) cmpName,
			/*RZ14000622- End*/	

			cmpNameEng, 
			cmpPreTel,
			cmpTel,
			cmpTelUpTo,
			cmpTelDesc,
			cmpPreTelII,
			cmpTelII,
			cmpTelUpToII,
			cmpFax, 
			(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country Co WHERE Co.CountryID = tmp.cmpCountryId) cmpCountry,  
			cmpProvinceId,
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province Pr WHERE Pr.ProvinceId = tmp.cmpProvinceId) cmpProvince,  
			cmpCityId,
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City Ci WHERE Ci.CityId = tmp.cmpCityId) cmpCity, 
			cmpAddress,
			cmpAddressDesc,
			cmpZipCode,
			cmpPostBox,
			cmpEmail,
			cmpWebSite,
			cmpDescription,
			*/
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					ISNULL(( SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleID = prsTitleId), '''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL( prsFName, '''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL( prsLName, '''')
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonTop1ByOrderView,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					(SELECT Jobs FROM dbo.CrmPersonJobs WHERE PersonJobID = prsJobId)
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonJobTop1ByOrderView,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					(SELECT PersonDepartment FROM dbo.CrmPersonDepartments WHERE PersonDepartmentID = prsDepartmentId) 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonDepartmanTop1ByOrderView,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					prsMobile 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonMobileTop1ByOrderView 
			/*RZ970425- Add cmpReferUser*/
			,(SELECT ISNULL([FName],'''') + '' '' + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.[UserId] = cmpReferUserId) [cmpReferUser]
			
		FROM 
		( ' +
		@txt +
		'
		) tmp /* ORDER BY 
		(SELECT top 1 cnvsdate FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
		WHERE cnvscompanyid = companyid
		AND cnvsstatusId = 22)*/
		'
	)
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN/*RZ960310 For multi language*/
	Exec   
	(
		'
		SELECT 
			*,
			/*
			add   * and
			cach in 9512 for return by number of prs
			DISTINCT*/
			/*
			CompanyID,
			cmpNumber,
			cmpTitleId,
			/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = tmp.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + ') cmpTitle,
			(SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = tmp.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + @LanguageID + ') cmpName,*/			
			/*RZ14000622- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(tmp.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = tmp.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID =  ' + @LanguageID + '),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID =  ' + @LanguageID + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = tmp.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) cmpTitle,
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(tmp.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = tmp.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID =  ' + @LanguageID + '),''''))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID =  ' + @LanguageID + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = tmp.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) cmpName,
			/*RZ14000622- End*/			
			cmpNameEng, 
			cmpPreTel,
			cmpTel,
			cmpTelUpTo,
			(SELECT cmpTelDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = tmp.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + @LanguageID + ') cmpTelDesc,
			cmpPreTelII,
			cmpTelII,
			cmpTelUpToII,
			cmpFax, 
			(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang Co WHERE Co.CountryID = tmp.cmpCountryId AND Co.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + ') cmpCountry,  
			cmpProvinceId,
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang Pr WHERE Pr.ProvinceId = tmp.cmpProvinceId AND Pr.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + ') cmpProvince,  
			cmpCityId,
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang Ci WHERE Ci.CityId = tmp.cmpCityId AND Ci.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + ') cmpCity, 
			(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = tmp.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + @LanguageID + ') cmpAddress,
			(SELECT cmpAddressDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = tmp.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + @LanguageID + ') cmpAddressDesc,
			cmpZipCode,
			cmpPostBox,
			cmpEmail,
			cmpWebSite,
			(SELECT cmpDescription FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = tmp.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + @LanguageID + ') cmpDescription,
			*/
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					ISNULL(( SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersonTitleID = prsTitleId AND PTLN.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + '), '''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID  = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + @LanguageID + '), '''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL( (SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID  = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + @LanguageID + '), '''')
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonTop1ByOrderView,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					(SELECT Jobs FROM dbo.CrmPersonJobs_Lang PJLN WHERE PJLN.PersonJobID = prsJobId  AND PJLN.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + ')
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonJobTop1ByOrderView,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					(SELECT PersonDepartment FROM dbo.CrmPersonDepartments_Lang PDLN WHERE PDLN.PersonDepartmentID = prsDepartmentId AND PDLN.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + ') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonDepartmanTop1ByOrderView,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					prsMobile 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId
				ORDER BY prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID  
			) PersonMobileTop1ByOrderView 
			/*RZ970425- Add cmpReferUser*/
			,(SELECT ISNULL([FName],'''') + '' '' + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.[UserId] = cmpReferUserId AND U.LanguageID =' + @LanguageID + ') [cmpReferUser]			
			/*RZ970425- because these fields (6) not insert in CRMsystempinfo in multi language*/
			,(SELECT [cmpCoordinateLatitude] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC1 WHERE CC1.[CompanyID]  = tmp.CompanyID) [cmpCoordinateLatitude]
			,(SELECT [cmpCoordinateLongitude] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC1 WHERE CC1.[CompanyID]  = tmp.CompanyID) [cmpCoordinateLongitude]
			,(SELECT [cmpCoordinateLastDate] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC1 WHERE CC1.[CompanyID]  = tmp.CompanyID) [cmpCoordinateLastDate]
			,(SELECT [cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC1 WHERE CC1.[CompanyID]  = tmp.CompanyID) [cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian]
			,(SELECT [cmpCoordinateZoomLevel] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC1 WHERE CC1.[CompanyID]  = tmp.CompanyID) [cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
			,(SELECT [cmpCoordinateLastUserId] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC1 WHERE CC1.[CompanyID]  = tmp.CompanyID) [cmpCoordinateLastUserId]
			/*RZ970425*/			
		FROM 
		( ' +
		@txt +
		'
		) tmp /* ORDER BY 
		(SELECT top 1 cnvsdate FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
		WHERE cnvscompanyid = companyid
		AND cnvsstatusId = 22)*/
		'
	)
END
/**/
END








GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspRpt_CompanyProjectAndCnvsInfoWithFilterData]
/* 
	Exec CRMspRpt_CompanyProjectAndCnvsInfoWithFilterData
*/
(
@Mode INT,/*mode= 1 ===> Report of the project information with date*/
@FromDate DATETIME,
@ToDate DATETIME,
@UserId INT, 
@rptDate NVARCHAR(50), 
@rptDay NVARCHAR(50),
@prsSel BIT, 
@prjSel BIT, 
@cnvsSel BIT, 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @Res VARCHAR(MAX), @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Term VARCHAR(MAX)= '', @TermI VARCHAR(MAX)= ''
SELECT @Res=Txt FROM CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
/**/
IF(@Res IS NOT NULL AND @Res <>'''')
BEGIN/* with filter data from company report*/
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND [prjCompanyId] IN (SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Res + ') T ) '

	IF(@prjSel =1)
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND REPLACE(CAST([prjTitle] as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) IN (SELECT REPLACE(CAST([prjTitle] as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) FROM (' + @Res + ') T ) '
END

IF(@Mode = 1) 
BEGIN
	/*Report of the project information with date*/
	/*SET @TermI = @TermI + ' AND [cnvsRemindDateTime] BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FromDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' +  CAST(@ToDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''*/
	SET @TermI = @TermI + ' AND CAST([cnvsRemindDateTime]  AS DATE)  <= ''' + CAST(CAST(@FromDate AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
END

/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	IF(@Mode = 1) /*Report of the project information with date*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = @Qry + 'SELECT (SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName , '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') rptUserName
		  ,''' + @rptDate + ''' rptDate,''' + @rptDay + ''' rptDay,* FROM( 
		  SELECT  [ProjectID],[prjCompanyId]
		  /*,(SELECT 
				ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = cmpTitleId),'''') + '' '' +
				ISNULL(cmpName ,'''')
			FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) Company*/

		/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		,(SELECT 
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
								ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
						FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
						WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
						)				
			 END)CompanyName				
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE C.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) Company
		/*RZ14000621- End*/			


		  ,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users u WHERE U.UserID = 
			(SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = prjCompanyId )) CompanyReferUser

		  ,(SELECT cmpCode FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) CompanyCode

			(SELECT cmpCountryId FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = prjCompanyId )) CompanyCountry
		  ,(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province P WHERE ProvinceID = 
			(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = prjCompanyId )) CompanyProvince

		  ,(SELECT PG.ProjectGroup FROM dbo.CrmProjectGroups_Lang PG WHERE PG.ProjectGroupID = [prjGroupId] AND PG.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [prjGroup]
		  ,[prjcnvsStatusGroupId]
		  ,(SELECT CS.ConversationStatusGroup FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups_Lang CS WHERE CS.ConversationStatusGroupID = [prjcnvsStatusGroupId] AND CS.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [prjcnvsStatusGroup]
		  ,[prjOrderView]
		  ,(SELECT [prjPostFix] FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProjectId = PRJ.ProjectID  AND PLN.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [prjPostFix]
		  ,PRJ.[FirstRegisterUserId] [prjFirstRegisterUserId]
		  ,(SELECT ISNULL( FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = PRJ.[FirstRegisterUserId] AND U.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [prjFirstRegisterUser] 
		  ,PRJ.[FirstRegisterDatePersian] [prjFirstRegisterDatePersian]
		  ,PRJ.[FirstRegisterDate] [prjFirstRegisterDate],[prjRemindDate],[prjRemindDateTime],[prjFOCmpCanSee],[prjIsTicket]
		  ,[prjUserDepartmentId]
		  ,(SELECT [Department] FROM [dbo].[UserDepartments_Lang] UD WHERE UD.[DepartmentID] = [prjUserDepartmentId] AND UD.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [prjUserDepartment]
		  ,[ConversationID],[cnvsProjectId],[cnvsCompanyId],[cnvsDate],[cnvsDay],[cnvsTime],[cnvsDateTime]'		  		  
		SET @Qry = @Qry + ',[cnvsTopicId]
		  ,(SELECT CT.Topic FROM dbo.CrmConversationTopics_Lang CT WHERE CT.ConversationTopicsID = [cnvsTopicId] AND CT.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsTopic]		  
		  ,(SELECT [cnvsSubject] FROM dbo.CrmConversation_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.ConversationID = CNVS.ConversationID  AND CLN.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsSubject]
		  ,(SELECT [cnvsDesc] FROM dbo.CrmConversation_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.ConversationID = CNVS.ConversationID  AND CLN.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsDesc]
		  ,[cnvsPersonId]
		  ,(SELECT 
				ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM CRMPersonTitle_Lang CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId  AND CPT.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +'), '''') +'' ''+
				ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''') FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonId  AND CPLN.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +'),'''') 
			FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP 
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = [cnvsPersonId]) Person
		  ,(SELECT prsMobile FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = [cnvsPersonId]) PersonMobile
		  ,(SELECT prsEmail FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = [cnvsPersonId]) PersonEmail
		  ,(SELECT prsEmailII FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = [cnvsPersonId]) PersonEmailII
		  /**/
		  ,[cnvsUserId],(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = [cnvsUserId] AND U.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsUser]
		  ,[cnvsReferUserId]
		  ,(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = [cnvsReferUserId] AND U.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsReferUser]
		  ,(SELECT [cnvsReferDesc] FROM dbo.CrmConversation_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.ConversationID = CNVS.ConversationID  AND CLN.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +')[cnvsReferDesc]
		  ,[cnvsStatusId]
		  ,(SELECT CT.[Status] FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatus_Lang CT WHERE CT.ConversationStatusID = [cnvsStatusId] AND CT.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsStatus]
		  ,[cnvsPriorityId]
		  ,(SELECT CP.[Priority] FROM dbo.CrmConversationPriority_Lang CP WHERE CP.ConversationPriorityID = [cnvsPriorityId] AND CP.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsPriority]
		  ,[cnvsRemindDate],[cnvsRemindTime],[cnvsRemindDateTime],[cnvsRemindOk],[cnvsIsBookMark] '
		  SET @Qry = @Qry + ' ,CNVS.[UserId]  [cnvsRegisterUserId]
		  ,(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = CNVS.[UserId] AND U.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsRegisterUser]
		  ,CNVS.[RegisterDate] [cnvsRegisterDate]
		  ,CNVS.[RegisterDatePersian] [cnvsRegisterDatePersian]
		  ,CNVS.[FirstRegisterUserId] [cnvsFirstRegisterUserId]
		  ,(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = CNVS.[FirstRegisterUserId] AND U.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +') [cnvsFirstRegisterUser]
		  ,CNVS.[FirstRegisterDatePersian] [cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian]
		  ,CNVS.[FirstRegisterDate] [cnvsFirstRegisterDate]
		  ,[cnvsRemindDateForAllarm],[cnvsRemindDateTimeForAllarm],[cnvsFOCmpCanSee]
		  ,[cnvsIsTicket],[cnvsCoordinateLatitude],[cnvsCoordinateLongitude],[cnvsCoordinateLastDate],[cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian],[cnvsTicketIsRead],[cnvsDoingTime],[cnvsIsCopy],0 prjId'
		  /*RZ14030223- Add fields*/
		  SET @Qry = @Qry + ' 
		  ,[cnvsDoingTimeSec]
		  ,CAST([cnvsDoingTimeFrom] AS NVARCHAR(8))[cnvsDoingTimeFrom]
		  ,CAST([cnvsDoingTimeTo] AS NVARCHAR(8))[cnvsDoingTimeTo]
		  ,[cnvsStatusResultId]
		  ,(SELECT TOP (1) [ResultTitle]  FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusResult_Lang] CSR WHERE CSR.[ConversationStatusResultId] = [cnvsStatusResultId]  AND CSR.LanguageID = '+ CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(10)) +')[cnvsStatusResult]
		  '
		  /*RZ14030223- End*/

	   SET @Qry = @Qry + ' FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]  PRJ LEFT JOIN 
	  [dbo].[CrmConversation] CNVS ON PRJ.ProjectID = CNVS.cnvsProjectId ' + @TermI + '
	  WHERE 1 = 1 AND ISNULL([prjIsFinished],0) = 0 '
		SET @Term = @Term + ' ORDER BY ISNULL(PRJ.prjOrderView, PRJ.ProjectID), CNVS.cnvsDateTime DESC '
		EXECUTE(@Qry + @Term)
	END	  	
END
/**/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspRpt_CompanyReportProjectInformatinPivot] | References permission/temp-filter machinery

/*ADBF950905*/
CREATE  PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspRpt_CompanyReportProjectInformatinPivot]
(			/*-- exec CRMspRpt_CompanyProjectInformatinPivot 3 */
@UserId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/*--*/
/*
DECLARE @cmpId INT 
SET @cmpId = 10097
*/

/*--*/
DECLARE @cmpRepQuery NVARCHAR(MAX)
SELECT @cmpRepQuery = Txt 
FROM CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE UserId = @UserId
AND TypeId = 1
/*--*/
DECLARE @cmpIdstr VARCHAR(MAX), @cmpPrjIdstr VARCHAR(MAX),
@txt NVARCHAR(max)
SET @txt = 
'
SELECT @cmpIdstr= STUFF((SELECT DISTINCT '','' + cast(companyid as varchar(200)) 
FROM ('+
@cmpRepQuery +')p
FOR XML PATH(''''), TYPE
).value(''.'', ''NVARCHAR(MAX)'') 
,1,1,'''') ,
@cmpPrjIdstr= STUFF((SELECT DISTINCT '','' + cast(projectId as varchar(200)) 
FROM ('+
@cmpRepQuery +')p
FOR XML PATH(''''), TYPE
).value(''.'', ''NVARCHAR(MAX)'') 
,1,1,'''') 
'
EXECUTE sp_executesql @txt, N'@cmpIdstr VARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT, @cmpPrjIdstr VARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT', 
@cmpIdstr=@cmpIdstr OUTPUT, @cmpPrjIdstr=@cmpPrjIdstr OUTPUT
SELECT @cmpIdstr , @cmpPrjIdstr
/*--*/

DECLARE 
@colId AS NVARCHAR(MAX)
,@colsIdWithName AS NVARCHAR(MAX)
,@txtCmpMoreField  AS NVARCHAR(MAX)
,@txtPrjMoreField  AS NVARCHAR(MAX)
,@query  AS NVARCHAR(MAX)
SELECT @colId = STUFF((SELECT DISTINCT ',' + QUOTENAME(fieldtitleid) 
                    FROM CrmProjectInformations
                    WHERE ProjectId IN (
							SELECT projectId FROM crmprojects WHERE prjCompanyId IN( @cmpIdstr) 
							)
                    GROUP BY ProjectId, fieldtitleid, ProjectInformationId
            FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE
            ).value('.', 'NVARCHAR(MAX)') 
			,1,1,'')      
SELECT @colsIdWithName = STUFF((SELECT DISTINCT ',' + QUOTENAME(fieldtitleid )+' AS '''+ 
				(SELECT FieldTitle FROM dbo.CrmProjectFieldTitle WHERE projectfieldtitleid = fieldtitleid)+''''
                    FROM CrmProjectInformations
                    WHERE ProjectId IN (
							SELECT projectId FROM crmprojects WHERE prjCompanyId IN( @cmpIdstr)
							)
                    GROUP BY fieldtitleid, ProjectInformationId
            FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE
            ).value('.', 'NVARCHAR(MAX)') 
			,1,1,'')  
/*--*/                            
SET @txtCmpMoreField = '  			
			(SELECT cmpNumber FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId) 	)cmpNumber,
			(SELECT cmpActivityType FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId) 	)cmpActivityType,			
			(SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups WHERE CompanyGroupID = 
					(SELECT cmpGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpGroup,
			(SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups WHERE CompanySubGroupID = 
					(SELECT cmpSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpSubGroup,						
			(SELECT GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII WHERE CompanyGroupIIID = 
					(SELECT cmpGroupIdII FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpGroupII,						
			(SELECT GroupIIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIII WHERE CompanyGroupIIIID = 
					(SELECT cmpGroupIdIII FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpGroupIII,
			(SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV WHERE CompanyGroupIVID = 
					(SELECT cmpGroupIdIV FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpGroupIV,
			(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = 
					(SELECT cmpTypeId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpType,																								
			(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = 
					(SELECT cmpPresentationId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpPresentation,
			(SELECT cmpCountryId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
				(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))cmpCountryId,
			(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryID = 
					(SELECT cmpCountryId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpCountry,
			(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
				(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))cmpProvinceId,						
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceID = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpProvince,
			(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
				(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))cmpCityId,						
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpCity,						 
			(SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
				(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))cmpReferUserId,						
			(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserID = 
					(SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )cmpReferUser
												 '
/*--*/						
SET @txtPrjMoreField = '
			(SELECT prjDesc FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjDesc,
			(SELECT prjStartDate FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjStartDate,
			(SELECT prjStartDay FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjStartDay,
			(SELECT prjStartTime FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjStartTime,
			(SELECT prjStartDateTime FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjStartDateTime,
			(SELECT prjStarterUserId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjStarterUserId,			
			(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserID = 
					(SELECT prjStarterUserId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))prjStarterUser,												
			(SELECT prjIsFinished FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjIsFinished,
			(SELECT prjFinishedDate FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjFinishedDate,
			(SELECT prjFinishedDay FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjFinishedDay,
			(SELECT prjFinishedTime FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjFinishedTime,
			(SELECT prjFinishedDateTime FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjFinishedDateTime,
			(SELECT prjFinishedUserId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjFinishedUserId,
			(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserID = 
					(SELECT prjFinishedUserId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))prjFinishedUser,								
			(SELECT prjGradeId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjGradeId,
			(SELECT ProjectGrade FROM dbo.CrmProjectGrade WHERE ProjectGradeID = 
					(SELECT prjGradeId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))prjGrade,								
			(SELECT prjPriorityId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjPriorityId,
			(SELECT Priority FROM dbo.CrmProjectPriority WHERE ProjectPriorityID = 
					(SELECT prjPriorityId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))prjPriority,								
			(SELECT prjFirstRegisterDate FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjFirstRegisterDate,
			(SELECT prjFirstRegisterUserId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjFirstRegisterUserId,
			(SELECT UserId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)PrjUserId,
			(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserID = 
					(SELECT pp.UserId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))PrjUser,							
			(SELECT Year1 FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)PrjYear1,
			(SELECT Month1 FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)PrjMonth1,
			(SELECT RegisterDate FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)PrjRegisterDate,
			(SELECT RegisterDatePersian FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)PrjRegisterDatePersian,
			(SELECT prjGroupId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjGroupId,
			(SELECT ProjectGroup FROM dbo.CrmProjectGroups WHERE ProjectGroupID = 
					(SELECT prjGroupId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))prjGroup,								
			(SELECT prjcnvsStatusGroupId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjcnvsStatusGroupId,
			(SELECT ConversationStatusGroup FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups WHERE ConversationStatusGroupID = 
					(SELECT prjcnvsStatusGroupId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId))prjcnvsStatusGroup,								
			(SELECT AppType FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)PrjAppType '			
/*--*/						
SET @query = N'SELECT ProjectId, 
			(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)prjCompanyId,
			(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId)) )CompanyTitle, 
			(SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = 
						(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId) 	)cmpName,
			 (SELECT prjTitle FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId) prjTitle,	
			 (SELECT prjPostFix FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId) prjPostFix, '	
			+ @colsIdWithName + ',' +
			@txtCmpMoreField + ',' +
			@txtPrjMoreField +
			N' from 
             (
                select  ProjectId, fieldvalue, fieldtitleid 
                from CrmProjectInformations p
                WHERE ProjectId IN (
							SELECT projectId FROM crmprojects WHERE prjCompanyId IN ' +  @cmpIdstr	 +' 
							)               
            ) x
            pivot 
            (
                max(fieldvalue)
                for fieldtitleid in (' + @colId + N')
            ) p 
            ORDER BY (SELECT prjOrderView FROM dbo.CrmProjects pp WHERE pp.projectid = p.ProjectId), ProjectId
            '
/*--*/
print (@query)
/*--*/        
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspSearchCompanyWithAllFields] | References permission/temp-filter machinery



/*--ADBF*/
create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSearchCompanyWithAllFields]
( 
/*
	exec CRMspSearchCompanyWithAllFields 3, 'cmpNumber', 125 
*/
@UserId INT
,@FieldName nvarchar(500)
,@Value  nvarchar(500)
)
As
BEGIN
/*-----*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId
/**/
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/**/
execute
(
'select * from CrmCompany C 
where 1=1 And ' 
+ @FieldName + ' = N''' + @Value +''' ' 
+ @cmpDataPermision
)
/*-----*/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspSelectAndSearchCompany] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*RZ9510ESP951116*/
/*RZ990504- Add Permission*/
/*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSelectAndSearchCompany]
/* 
	exec CRMspSelectAndSearchCompany N'va',3,1033,1065   
*/
(
@Val NVARCHAR(50),
@UserId INT = NULL,/*RZ990504- Add Permission*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*RZ990504- Add Permission*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX)
/*RZ990504-End*/
/*-------------------------*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	/*RZ990504- Add Permission*/
	SET @Qry = '
	SELECT 
	[CompanyID]
      ,[ImportDate]
      ,[ImportDateTime]
      ,[cmpNumber]
      /*,[cmpName]*/
	  /*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	  ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		   THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
		   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) [cmpName]
	  /*RZ14000615- End*/
      ,[cmpNameEng]
      ,[cmpTitleId]
      ,[cmpFame]
      ,[cmpActivityType]
      ,[cmpGroupId]
      ,[cmpSubGroupId]
      ,[cmpGroupIdII]
      ,[cmpGroupIdIII]
      ,[cmpGroupIdIV]
      ,[cmpTypeId]
      ,[cmpPresentationId]
      ,[cmpPreTel]
      ,[cmpTel]
      ,[cmpTelUpTo]
      ,[cmpTelDesc]
      ,[cmpPreTelII]
      ,[cmpTelII]
      ,[cmpTelUpToII]
      ,[cmpTelIIDesc]
      ,[cmpMobile]
      ,[cmpFax]
      ,[cmpFaxDesc]
      ,[cmpCountryId]
      ,[cmpProvinceId]
      ,[cmpCityId]
      ,[cmpAddress]
      ,[cmpAddressDesc]
      ,[cmpZipCode]
      ,[cmpPostBox]
      ,[cmpWebSite]
      ,[cmpEmail]
      ,[cmpInteresting]
      ,[cmpSpecialPoint]
      ,[cmpDescription]
      ,[cmpUserName]
      ,[cmpPassword]
      ,[cmpFirstRegisterUserId]
      ,[UserId]
      ,[Year1]
      ,[Month1]
      ,[RegisterDate]
      ,[RegisterDatePersian]
      ,[AddType]
      ,[cmpRegistrationNumber]
      ,[cmpEconomicCode]
      ,[cmpNationalCode]
      ,[GUId]
      ,[cmpBuyLimit]
      ,[cmpAccountNumber]
      ,[cmpAccInquiry]
      ,[cmpFreight]
      ,[cmpInquiry]
      ,[cmpReferUserId]
      ,[AppType]
      ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI]
      ,[cmpCode]
      ,[cmpEffectiveDatePersian]
      ,[cmpEffectiveDateTime]
      ,[LanguageID]
      ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
      --,[FirstRegisterDateTime] /*ESP951116*/
	  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian] /*ESP951116*/
      ,[FirstRegisterDate] 
	  /*,ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') 
	  + '' '' + ISNULL(cmpName, '''') TitleCompany,*/
	  /*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	  ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
				  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
		   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		 END)TitleCompany,
	  /*RZ14000615- End*/
      (SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId) Country,
      (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId) Province,
      (SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId) City	
	FROM dbo.CrmCompany C'
	/*WHERE (cmpName LIKE (''%''+@Val+''%') OR cmpNameEng LIKE ('%'+@Val+'%') OR cmpNumber LIKE ('%'+@Val+'%') )'*/

	SET @Qry =  @Qry + ' WHERE (
	(REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like ''%' + REPLACE(CAST(@Val as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))  +'%'')'+
	'OR (REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpNameEng as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like ''%' + REPLACE(CAST(@Val as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))  +'%'')'+
	'OR cmpNumber LIKE (''%'+@Val+'%''))' 
	/*RZ990504- End*/
	/*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)  AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  Like ''%' + REPLACE(CAST(@Val as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))  +'%'')'
	/*RZ14000615- End*/
	
	
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	/*RZ990504- Add Permission*/
	SET @Qry = '
		SELECT  
	   [CompanyID]
      ,[ImportDate]
      ,[ImportDateTime]
      ,[cmpNumber]
      /*,(SELECT cmpName FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' ) as [cmpName]*/
	  /*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	  ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			   THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),''''))
			   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
		 END) [cmpName]
	  /*RZ14000615- End*/
      ,[cmpNameEng]
      ,[cmpTitleId]
      ,(SELECT cmpFame FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpFame]
      ,(SELECT cmpActivityType FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpActivityType]
      ,[cmpGroupId]
      ,[cmpSubGroupId]

      ,[cmpGroupIdIII]
      ,[cmpGroupIdIV]
      ,[cmpTypeId]

      ,[cmpPreTelII]
      ,[cmpTelII]
      ,[cmpTelUpToII]

      ,[cmpProvinceId]
      ,[cmpCityId]
      ,(SELECT cmpAddress FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpAddress]
      ,(SELECT cmpAddressDesc FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpAddressDesc]
      ,[cmpZipCode]
      ,[cmpPostBox]
      ,[cmpWebSite]
      ,[cmpEmail]
      ,(SELECT cmpInteresting FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpInteresting]
      ,(SELECT cmpSpecialPoint FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID =' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpSpecialPoint]
      ,(SELECT cmpDescription FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpDescription]
      ,[cmpUserName]
      ,[cmpPassword]
      ,[cmpFirstRegisterUserId]
      ,[UserId]

      ,[Month1]

      ,[cmpReferUserId]
      ,[AppType]
      ,(SELECT cmpSpecialWordForCI FROM CrmCompany_Lang WHERE  CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') as [cmpSpecialWordForCI]
      ,[cmpCode]
      ,[cmpEffectiveDatePersian]
      ,[cmpEffectiveDateTime]
      ,[LanguageID]
      ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
      --,[FirstRegisterDateTime] /*ESP951116*/
	  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian] /*ESP951116*/
      ,[FirstRegisterDate]

        	, '''') + '' '' +         	
        	ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(cmpName, '''')FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''')
        	TitleCompany*/

	  /*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	  ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
					  ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
			   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP

      ,ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(Country, '''')FROM dbo.Country_Lang CtyL WHERE CtyL.CountryId = C.cmpCountryId AND CtyL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') Country   	        
      ,ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(Province, '''')FROM dbo.Province_Lang PL WHERE PL.ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId AND PL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') Province   	         
      ,ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(City, '''')FROM dbo.City_Lang CitL WHERE CitL.CityId = C.cmpCityId AND CitL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') City	         
	FROM dbo.CrmCompany C'
	/*WHERE
	((c.CompanyID in (SELECT CompanyID FROM CrmCompany_Lang CLang WHERE CLang.cmpName LIKE ('%'+@Val+'%') AND CLang.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ')) 
	OR cmpNameEng LIKE ('%'+@Val+'%') OR cmpNumber LIKE ('%'+@Val+'%') )*/



	SET @Qry =  @Qry + ' WHERE (
	c.CompanyID in (SELECT CompanyID FROM CrmCompany_Lang CLang WHERE (REPLACE(CAST(CLang.cmpName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE (''%'+REPLACE(CAST(@Val as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))+'%'') AND CLang.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '))' 	+
	'OR (REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpNameEng as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like ''%' + REPLACE(CAST(@Val as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))  +'%'')'+
	'OR cmpNumber LIKE (''%'+@Val+'%''))' +
	/*RZ990504- End*/
	/*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/	
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
												 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)  AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  Like ''%' + REPLACE(CAST(@Val as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610))  +'%'')'
	/*RZ14000615- End*/
	
/**/
END
/*-------------------------*/
/*RZ990504- Add Permission*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId
/*RZ990504-End*/
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
EXECUTE ( @Qry + @cmpDataPermision)
/*--*/
DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 10
AND UserId = @UserId
/**/	
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyEmailsAndMobilesFromRptFilterData] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*RZ981120*/
/*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*RZ14001120-Change first person to cmpMainPerson*/
/*RZ14010429- Add MobNumLen*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyEmailsAndMobilesFromRptFilterData]
(
/* 
	exec CRMspSelectCompanyEmailsAndMobilesFromRptFilterData 3,1,0, 1033, 1065
*/
@UserId INT,
@Type INT,/*@Type = 1 ==> Email, @Type = 2 ==> Mobile*/
@Mode INT,/*@Mode = 0 ==> All, @Mode = 1 ==> Correct, @Mode = 2 ==> Incorrect*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL  
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*========================================================*/
/*RZ14010429- Add MobNumLen*/
DECLARE @MobNumLen  INT,@MobNumLenNoCheck BIT
SELECT TOP (1)
	 @MobNumLen = [MobNumLen]
    ,@MobNumLenNoCheck = [MobNumLenNoCheck]      
FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

SET @MobNumLen = ISNULL(@MobNumLen,1)
SET @MobNumLenNoCheck = ISNULL(@MobNumLenNoCheck,0)
/*RZ14010429- End*/
/*========================================================*/
DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*========================================================*/
SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
END
/*========================================================*/
IF(@Type = 1)/*Email*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(@Mode = 0)/*ALL*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ISNULL(CC.cmpEmail,'''') <> '''' '				
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 1)/*Correct*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND (
				(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%_@_%_.__%'') AND
				((CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%"%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%<%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%>%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%(%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%)%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%[%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%]%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%;%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%:%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%,%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%"%'') AND
				(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%/%''))
			) '
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*Incorrect*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND 
					(
						(CC.cmpEmail NOT LIKE ''%_@_%_.__%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%"%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%<%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%>%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%(%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%)%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%[%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%]%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%;%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%:%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%,%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%"%'') OR
						(CC.cmpEmail LIKE ''%/%'')
					) '
	END
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@Type = 2)/*Mobile*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(@Mode = 0)/*ALL*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ISNULL(CC.cmpMobile,'''') <> '''' '				
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 1)/*Correct*/
	BEGIN
		/*SET @Term = @Term + ' AND (LEN(CC.cmpMobile) = 11
					AND LEFT(CC.cmpMobile, 2) = ''09'') '*/
		/*RZ14010429- Add MobNumLen*/		
		SET @Term = @Term + 'AND (
								((' + CAST(@MobNumLenNoCheck AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = 0 ) 
									 AND 
									(LEN(CC.cmpMobile) = ' + CAST(@MobNumLen AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LEFT(CC.cmpMobile, 2) = ''09''))
								  OR 
									(' + CAST(@MobNumLenNoCheck AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = 1)
								) '
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ISNULL(CC.cmpMobile,'''') <> '''' '
		/*RZ14010429- End*/
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*Incorrect*/
	BEGIN
		/*SET @Term = @Term + ' AND (LEN(CC.cmpMobile) <> 11
		AND LEFT(CC.cmpMobile, 2) <> ''09'') '*/

		/*RZ14010429- Add MobNumLen*/		
		SET @Term = @Term + 'AND (
								((' + CAST(@MobNumLenNoCheck AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' = 0 ) 
									 AND 
									(LEN(CC.cmpMobile) <> ' + CAST(@MobNumLen AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' OR LEFT(CC.cmpMobile, 2) <> ''09''))								 
								) '
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ISNULL(CC.cmpMobile,'''') <> '''' '
		/*RZ14010429- End*/

	END
/**/
END
/*========================================================*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @Qry = @Qry + 'SELECT DISTINCT 
	CC.CompanyID,	
	/*LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleId = CC.cmpTitleId),'''') + '' '' +
		ISNULL(cmpName,''''), ''''))) CompanyName,
	LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(cmpName, ''''))) CmpName,
	LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleId = CC.cmpTitleId), ''''))) cmpTitle,*/

	/*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(CC.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)
	,''''))) CompanyName,

	LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL(CC.[cmpName],''''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP

		ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			)

	LTRIM(RTRIM(CC.cmpEmail)) Email, 
	LEN(CC.cmpEmail) EmailLength, 
	(CASE WHEN CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) > 0 THEN 
		SUBSTRING(CC.cmpEmail, 1, CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) - 1) 
	ELSE
		''!!Error In Email Syntax''
	END) EmailPartI,
	(CASE WHEN (CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) > 0 And CHARINDEX(''.'', CC.cmpEmail) > 0 ) THEN 
		SUBSTRING(CC.cmpEmail, 
			CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) + 1, 
			CHARINDEX(''.'', 
				SUBSTRING(CC.cmpEmail, 
							CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) + 2, 
							LEN(CC.cmpEmail)- CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail)
						)
				) 
				) 
	ELSE 			
		''!!Error In Email Syntax'' 
	END) EmailPartII, 
	(CASE WHEN (CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) > 0 And CHARINDEX(''.'', CC.cmpEmail) > 0 ) THEN

			CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) + 1 +
			(CHARINDEX(''.'', 
				SUBSTRING(CC.cmpEmail, 
							CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail)+2, 
							LEN(CC.cmpEmail)- CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail)
						)
				) +1
				)  
			,	
			LEN( SUBSTRING(CC.cmpEmail, 
							CHARINDEX( ''@'', CC.cmpEmail) + 1, 
							LEN(CC.cmpEmail)- CHARINDEX(''@'', CC.cmpEmail) ))
				) 
	ELSE 			
		''!!Error In Email Syntax''

FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
WHERE 1 = 1 
AND ISNULL(CC.cmpMainPersonId,0) > 0 ' /*RZ14001120-Change first person to cmpMainPerson*/

-- [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyFilesFromRptFilterData] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*RZ14001215*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyFilesFromRptFilterData]
(	
/* 
	exec [CRMspSelectCompanyFilesFromRptFilterData]  3, 1033, 1065 
*/
@UserId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*========================================================*/
DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*========================================================*/
SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
END
/*========================================================*/
DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
		[CompanyId] INT
)
/*========================================================*/
SET @Qry = @Qry + N'SELECT DISTINCT 
						CC.CompanyID	
					FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
					WHERE 1 = 1 					
					'
/*EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt)*/
IF(ISNULL(@Qry + @Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([CompanyId])
	EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt)
END
/*========================================================*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/	
	SELECT 
		 [FileDirectoryID]
		,[FileDirectoryTypeId]
		,[MasterId]
		,[DetailId]
		,[SubDetailId]
		,[Title]
		,[FolderName]
		,[FileName]
		,[FilePath]		
		,[TypeOprObjectId]		
		,[TypeOprObjectIdI]
		,[TypeOprObjectIdII]		
	FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory] F
	WHERE [MasterId] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] FROM @TmpTable)
	AND FileDirectoryTypeId NOT IN(-110)/*CrmCmsNews*/
	ORDER BY [OrderView]
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [FileDirectoryID]
		,[FileDirectoryTypeId]
		,[MasterId]
		,[DetailId]
		,[SubDetailId]
		,(SELECT FLN.[Title] FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang] FLN WHERE FLN.FileDirectoryID = F.FileDirectoryID AND FLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID)[Title]
		,[FolderName]
		,[FileName]
		,[FilePath]		
		,[TypeOprObjectId]		
		,[TypeOprObjectIdI]
		,[TypeOprObjectIdII]		
	FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory] F
	WHERE [MasterId] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] FROM @TmpTable)
	AND FileDirectoryTypeId NOT IN(-110)/*CrmCmsNews*/
	ORDER BY [OrderView]
/**/
END
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyLocationFromRptFilterData] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*RZ960806*/
/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyLocationFromRptFilterData]
(           
/*
	exec CRMspSelectCompanyLocationFromRptFilterData 3, 1033, 1065
*/ 
@UserId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @Res VARCHAR(MAX)
SELECT @Res=Txt FROM CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN		         
		EXECUTE(     
		'SELECT 
			C.[CompanyID],
			C.cmpNumber,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM USERS u where u.UserId = C.cmpReferUserId) cmpReferUser,
			/*ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle ct WHERE ct.CompanyTitleId = cmpTitleId),'''') + '' '' +
			C.cmpName CompanyName,*/
			/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) CompanyName,
			/*RZ14000621- End*/	
			C.[cmpCoordinateLatitude],
			C.[cmpCoordinateLongitude],
			C.[cmpCoordinateLastDate],
			(SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDate](CAST(C.[cmpCoordinateLastDate] AS DATE))) cmpCoordinateLastDatePerWithSlash,
			C.[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel],
			C.[cmpAddressDesc],
			ISNULL((SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country Co WHERE Co.CountryId = C.cmpCountryId),'''') + 
			ISNULL(''-'' + (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province P WHERE P.ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId),'''') +
			ISNULL(''-'' + (SELECT City FROM City Ci WHERE Ci.CityId = C.cmpCityId) + ''-'','''') + 
			C.[cmpAddress] CompanyAddress				 
		 FROM dbo.CrmCompany C ' +
		'WHERE [cmpCoordinateLatitude] IS NOT NULL AND [cmpCoordinateLongitude] IS NOT NULL
		 AND CompanyID IN ( '+
		'SELECT CompanyId FROM ( '+@Res+') T ) '+   
		'ORDER BY cmpNumber Desc '
		)	
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN		
		EXECUTE(     
		'SELECT 
			C.[CompanyID],
			C.cmpNumber,			
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM Users_Lang u WHERE u.UserId = C.cmpReferUserId AND u.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + ') cmpReferUser,
			/*ISNULL(( SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang ct WHERE ct.CompanyTitleId = C.cmpTitleId AND ct.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + '),'''')
				+ '' '' +
				ISNULL((SELECT CL.cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID + '), C.cmpName)
			CompanyName,*/

			/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + '),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + '),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + '),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) CompanyName,
			/*RZ14000621- End*/

			C.[cmpCoordinateLatitude],
			C.[cmpCoordinateLongitude],
			C.[cmpCoordinateLastDate],
			(SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDate](CAST(C.[cmpCoordinateLastDate] AS DATE))) cmpCoordinateLastDatePerWithSlash,
			C.[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel],
			(SELECT CL.cmpAddressDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID + ') cmpAddressDesc,  	  	  	  	 
			ISNULL((SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang Co WHERE Co.CountryId = C.cmpCountryId AND Co.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + '),'''') + 
			ISNULL(''-'' + (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang P WHERE P.ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId AND P.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + '),'''') +
			ISNULL(''-'' + (SELECT City FROM City_Lang Ci WHERE Ci.CityId = C.cmpCityId AND Ci.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID  + ') + ''-'' ,'''') +  
			(SELECT CL.cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' +  @LanguageID + ') CompanyAddress			 
		 FROM dbo.CrmCompany C ' +
		'WHERE [cmpCoordinateLatitude] IS NOT NULL AND [cmpCoordinateLongitude] IS NOT NULL
		 AND CompanyID IN ( '+
		'SELECT CompanyId FROM ( '+@Res+') T ) '+   
		'ORDER BY cmpNumber Desc '
		)

-- [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyQueryText] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*ADBF9302940810ESP951029*/
/*ESP951223 For Status Cartable Error*/
/*RZ13960612- Add PersonTop1Order*/
/*ADBF 970525 Add Orderby*/
/*RZ970525 add cmpTitle*/
/*RZ990431- Add Fileds*/
/*NKR14010810- Optimize Sp Speed*/
/*NKR14010906- Add Field*/
/*NKR14011209- Change TopCnt From 100 To 500*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyQueryText]
(	
	/*
		exec CRMspSelectCompanyQueryText 0, 3, '', '13930303', 3, '', 0, '', ''  
	*/
	@CompanyId INT, 
	@UserId INT,
	@Mode VARCHAR(100),
	@DateParam VARCHAR(10), /*ForCartableS*/
	@SpecialUserId INT, /*ForCartableS*/
	@SortFields VARCHAR(1000), /*ForCartableS*/
	@tmpVal VARCHAR(1000), /*ForCartableS Status*/
	@Topcnt VARCHAR(10),
	@Sqltxt VARCHAR(max) OUTPUT ,
	@OnlyIds BIT = NULL,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL /*ESP13950724*/
)
AS
BEGIN 
	/**/
	DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
	Declare @SubQueryForenKeyTitle VARCHAR(max)

	SET @SubQueryForenKeyTitle = '
			(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle ct WHERE ct.CompanyTitleId = cmpTitleId) cmpTitle, 
			(SELECT Presentation FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation CP WHERE CP.CompanyPresentationID =   C.cmpPresentationId) cmpPresentation,
			(SELECT CompanyTypeName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyType CT WHERE CT.CompanyTypeId =   C.cmpTypeId) cmpType,   
			(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country Co WHERE Co.CountryId = C.cmpCountryId) cmpCountry,  
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province P WHERE P.ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId) cmpProvince,  
			(SELECT City FROM City Ci WHERE Ci.CityId = C.cmpCityId) cmpCity,  
			(SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupId =   C.cmpGroupId) cmpGroup,      
			(SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups SSB WHERE SSB.CompanySubGroupID =   C.cmpSubGroupId) cmpSubGroup,      
			(SELECT GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII CGII WHERE CGII.CompanyGroupIIId =   C.cmpGroupIdII) cmpGroupII,      
			(SELECT GroupIIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIII CGIII WHERE CGIII.CompanyGroupIIIId =   C.cmpGroupIdIII) cmpGroupIII,      
			(SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV CGIV WHERE CGIV.CompanyGroupIVId =   C.cmpGroupIdIV) cmpGroupIV,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM USERS u where u.UserId = C.cmpReferUserId) cmpReferUser,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM USERS u where u.UserId = C.cmpSuppUserId) cmpSuppUser,/*NKR14010906*/
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM USERS u where u.UserId = C.UserId) cmpUser,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM USERS u where u.UserId = C.cmpFirstRegisterUserId) cmpFirstRegisterUser
			,(SELECT TOP 1
				ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId), '''') + '' ''+
				ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
			ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID)) PersonTop1Order
			,(SELECT TOP 1
				ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId), '''') + '' ''+
				ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
			AND CompanyPersonId = C.cmpMainPersonId) cmpMainPerson
		  '

	DECLARE @SearchField VARCHAR(max) = ' C.*, ' + @SubQueryForenKeyTitle
	/**/
	IF (ISNULL(@Topcnt, '') = '')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SELECT @Topcnt = W.[Value] FROM CrmWebSetting W WHERE W.[KEY] = N'TopCountCompany'
		/**/
	END
	/**/
	IF (ISNULL(@Topcnt, '') = '' OR @Topcnt = N'null' OR @Topcnt = N'NULL' OR @Topcnt = '0')
	BEGIN
		SET @Topcnt = N'500'
	END
	/**/
	/**/
	/**/
	/**/
	IF @Mode = 'Search'
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @SearchTxt VARCHAR(max), 
				@SearchTxtObjType NVARCHAR(200),
				@SearchTxtJsResult NVARCHAR(MAX)
		
		SELECT 
			@SearchTxt = TXT ,
			@SearchTxtObjType = TXTObjType,
			@SearchTxtJsResult = TXTJsResult
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 10 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
		/**/
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		SET @Sqltxt = ' Select Top ' + @Topcnt + ' ' + @SearchField + ' '  + @SearchTxt + ' '  + @cmpDataPermision + ' ORDER BY CompanyId DESC, cmpTitle'
		/**/
	END 
	ELSE IF @Mode = 'Remind'
	BEGIN
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID

		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId

		DECLARE @RemindTxt VARCHAR(max)

		IF @Topcnt > 0 
			SET @RemindTxt = ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
							 ' inner join CRMResultsReminderCartable('+ @DateParam + 
							 ', ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+ ' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID ' 
		ELSE
			SET @RemindTxt =	' SELECT  ' + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
								' inner join CRMResultsReminderCartable('+ @DateParam + 
								', ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+ ' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID '   

		IF @SpecialUserId > 0  SET @RemindTxt = @RemindTxt + ' WHERE F.cnvsReferUserId = ' + CAST(@SpecialUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) 

		SET @RemindTxt = @RemindTxt +@cmpDataPermision	

		IF @SortFields <> '' SET @RemindTxt = @RemindTxt + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc' 

		SET @Sqltxt = @RemindTxt
		/**/
	END 
	ELSE IF @Mode = 'Daily'
	BEGIN
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
		
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId
		
		DECLARE @DailyTxt VARCHAR(max)

		IF @Topcnt > 0 
			SET @DailyTxt = ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
							' inner join CRMResultsDailyCartable('+ @DateParam + 
							', ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+ ' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID ' 
		ELSE
			SET @DailyTxt = ' SELECT  ' + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
							' inner join CRMResultsDailyCartable('+ @DateParam + 
							', ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+ ' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID '   

		IF @SpecialUserId > 0 SET @DailyTxt = @DailyTxt + ' WHERE F.cnvsUserID = ' + CAST(@SpecialUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) 

		SET @DailyTxt = @DailyTxt +@cmpDataPermision		

		IF @SortFields <> '' SET @DailyTxt = @DailyTxt + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc' 
		/**/
		SET @Sqltxt = @DailyTxt 
		/**/
	END 
	ELSE IF @Mode = 'Status'
	BEGIN
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID

		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId
		
		DECLARE @StatusTxt VARCHAR(max)

		IF @Topcnt > 0 
			SET @StatusTxt = ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
							 ' inner join CRMResultsStatusCartable('+ CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+ 
							 ' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID '
		ELSE
			SET @StatusTxt = ' SELECT  ' + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
							 ' inner join CRMResultsStatusCartable(' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) +
							 ' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID '

		IF @SpecialUserId > 0 SET @StatusTxt = @StatusTxt + ' WHERE F.cnvsReferUserId = ' + CAST(@SpecialUserId AS VARCHAR(10))

		SET @StatusTxt = @StatusTxt + '  AND  CAST(cnvsReferUserId AS VARCHAR(50)) +''9000''+ CAST(' 
		SET @StatusTxt = @StatusTxt + 'cnvsStatusId AS VARCHAR(50)) = '''+ @tmpVal + ''''
		SET @StatusTxt = @StatusTxt +@cmpDataPermision		

		IF @SortFields <> '' SET @StatusTxt = @StatusTxt + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc' 

		SET @Sqltxt = @StatusTxt
		/**/
	END 
	ELSE IF @Mode = 'BookMark'
	BEGIN
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID

		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId

		DECLARE @BookMarkTxt VARCHAR(max)

		IF @Topcnt > 0 
			SET @BookMarkTxt =  ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
								' inner join CRMResultsBookMarksCartable('+ CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+ 
								' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID ' 
		ELSE 
			SET @BookMarkTxt =  ' SELECT  ' + @SearchField + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C '+ 
								' inner join CRMResultsBookMarksCartable('+ CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+ 
								' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID '   
		IF @SpecialUserId > 0 SET @BookMarkTxt = @BookMarkTxt + ' WHERE F.cnvsReferUserId = ' + CAST(@SpecialUserId AS VARCHAR(10))

		SET @BookMarkTxt = @BookMarkTxt +@cmpDataPermision	

		IF @SortFields <> '' SET @BookMarkTxt = @BookMarkTxt + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc' 

		SET @Sqltxt = @BookMarkTxt
		/**/
	END 
	ELSE If @CompanyID = 0 AND @Mode = ''
	BEGIN 
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
		/**/
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
		/**/
		IF @Topcnt > 0 SET @txt = ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + ' From CrmCompany C Where 1=1 '
		ELSE SET @txt = ' SELECT ' + @SearchField + ' From CrmCompany C Where 1=1 '
		/**/
		IF @SortFields <> '' SET @Sqltxt = @txt + @cmpDataPermision + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc'
		ELSE SET @Sqltxt = @txt + @cmpDataPermision
		/**/
	END 
	ELSE If @CompanyID > 0 AND @Mode = ''
	BEGIN 
	/**/
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
		/**/
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		DECLARE @txt2 VARCHAR(max)
		/**/
		IF @Topcnt > 0 SET @txt2 = ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + ' From CrmCompany C Where CompanyID = ' + Cast(@CompanyID As varchar(10))+  @cmpDataPermision 
		ELSE SET @txt2 = ' SELECT  ' + @SearchField + ' From CrmCompany C Where CompanyID = ' + Cast(@CompanyID As varchar(10))+  @cmpDataPermision 			
		/**/
		SET @Sqltxt = @txt2
	/**/
	END 
END





GO

	@Topcnt VARCHAR(10),
	@Sqltxt VARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT ,
	@OnlyIds BIT = NULL,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX)
Declare @SubQueryForenKeyTitle VARCHAR(MAX)
set @SubQueryForenKeyTitle = '
  ( SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang ct WHERE ct.CompanyTitleId = C.cmpTitleId AND ct.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') cmpTitle,

  ( SELECT GroupIIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIII_Lang CGIII WHERE CGIII.CompanyGroupIIIId = C.cmpGroupIdIII AND CGIII.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') cmpGroupIII, 
  ( SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV_Lang CGIV WHERE CGIV.CompanyGroupIVId = C.cmpGroupIdIV AND CGIV.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') cmpGroupIV,
  ( SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM Users_Lang u where u.UserId = C.cmpReferUserId AND u.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') cmpReferUser,
  ( SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM Users_Lang u where u.UserId = C.cmpSuppUserId AND u.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') cmpSuppUser,/*NKR14010906*/
  ( SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM Users_Lang u where u.UserId = C.UserId AND u.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') cmpUser,
  ( SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM Users_Lang u where u.UserId = C.cmpFirstRegisterUserId AND u.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') cmpFirstRegisterUser
  /*RZ13960612- Add PersonTop1Order*/
  ,(SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang pl WHERE pl.PersonTitleId = P.prsTitleId AND pl.LanguageID =  ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') , '''') + '' ''+
	  ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM CrmCompanyPersons_Lang pl WHERE pl.CompanyPersonID= P.CompanyPersonID AND pl.LanguageID =  ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') , '''') + '' '' + 
	  ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM CrmCompanyPersons_Lang pl WHERE pl.CompanyPersonID= P.CompanyPersonID AND pl.LanguageID =  ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID)  + ') , '''')  
	 FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P
	WHERE P.prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
	ORDER BY ISNULL(P.prsOrderView, P.CompanyPersonID)) PersonTop1Order
 '
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @SearchField VARCHAR(MAX)
SET @SearchField = '
		(SELECT CL.cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') 
cmpName,  
		(SELECT CL.cmpFame FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') 
cmpFame,

@LanguageID) + ') cmpActivityType,

+ ') cmpTelIIDesc,  	  
		(SELECT CL.cmpFaxDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID) + 
') cmpFaxDesc,  	  	  
		(SELECT CL.cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID) + 
') cmpAddress,  	  	  	  
		(SELECT CL.cmpAddressDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), 
@LanguageID) + ') cmpAddressDesc,  	  	  	  	 
		(SELECT CL.cmpInteresting FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10), 
@LanguageID) + ') cmpInteresting,  	  	  	  	 	  
		(SELECT CL.cmpSpecialPoint FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = ' + convert(nvarchar(10),

		,C.[cmpMainPersonId]

   		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang pl WHERE pl.PersonTitleId = P.prsTitleId AND pl.LanguageID =  ' + convert(nvarchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') , '''') + '' ''+

		FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P
		WHERE P.prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
		AND CompanyPersonId = C.cmpMainPersonId) cmpMainPerson
		/*RZ990431- End*/
		,[cmpSuppUserId]/*NKR14010915*/

'

SET @SearchField =  @SearchField + ' , ' + @SubQueryForenKeyTitle
IF (@OnlyIds is not NULL)
Begin
	IF (@OnlyIds = 1)			/*RZ970525 add cmpTitle*/
		SET @SearchField = ' C.CompanyID, ( SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang ct WHERE ct.CompanyTitleId = C.cmpTitleId AND

IF @SpecialUserId > 0

  ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + '

SET @BookMarkTxt = 
  ' SELECT  ' + @SearchField + '

  ' ) F on C.CompanyID = F.CompanyID '   
IF @SpecialUserId > 0 
	SET @BookMarkTxt = @BookMarkTxt + ' WHERE F.cnvsReferUserId = ' + CAST(@SpecialUserId AS VARCHAR(10))
SET @BookMarkTxt = @BookMarkTxt +@cmpDataPermision	
IF @SortFields <> '' 
SET @BookMarkTxt = @BookMarkTxt + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc' 
/*--*/
/*--EXECUTE( @BookMarkTxt)*/
SET @Sqltxt = @BookMarkTxt
END 
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
ELSE 
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
If @CompanyID = 0 AND @Mode = ''
BEGIN 
/*--*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
/*--*/
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
IF @Topcnt > 0 
SET @txt = ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + '  
  From CrmCompany C Where 1=1 '
ELSE
SET @txt = ' SELECT ' + @SearchField + '  
  From CrmCompany C Where 1=1 '
/*--*/
IF @SortFields <> ''
/*--EXECUTE (@txt + @cmpDataPermision + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc')*/
SET @Sqltxt = @txt + @cmpDataPermision + ' Order By '+ @SortFields + ' Desc'
ELSE
/*--EXECUTE (@txt + @cmpDataPermision )*/
SET @Sqltxt = @txt + @cmpDataPermision
END 
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
ELSE 
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
If @CompanyID > 0 AND @Mode = ''
BEGIN 
/*--*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
/*--*/
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
DECLARE @txt2 VARCHAR(max)
IF @Topcnt > 0 
SET @txt2 = ' SELECT Top ' + @Topcnt + @SearchField + '  
 From CrmCompany C Where CompanyID = ' + 
			Cast(@CompanyID As varchar(10))+  @cmpDataPermision 
ELSE
SET @txt2 = ' SELECT  ' + @SearchField + ' 
  From CrmCompany C Where CompanyID = ' + 
			Cast(@CompanyID As varchar(10))+  @cmpDataPermision 			
/*--EXECUTE ( @txt2)*/
SET @Sqltxt = @txt2
END 
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
END

/*RZ14010322*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSelectCompanyToSendNotifFromRptFilterData]
(
/* 
	exec CRMspSelectCompanyToSendNotifFromRptFilterData 3,1033,1065			
*/
@UserId INT,
@LanguageID INT,
@DefaultLanguageID INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*======================================================================*/
/*==================== Define variables ==================*/
DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*========================================================*/
SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
END
/*========================================================*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @Qry = @Qry + 'SELECT DISTINCT
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(CC.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		,[cmpMainPersonId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
				 ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
		  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
		 ) [cmpMainPerson]
		,[cmpEmail]
		,[cmpMobile]		
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC  
	WHERE 1 = 1 '
	
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @Qry = @Qry + 'SELECT DISTINCT
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = CC.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName	
		,[cmpMainPersonId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
				 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
		  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
		 ) [cmpMainPerson]
		,[cmpEmail]
		,[cmpMobile]		
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] CC  
	WHERE 1 = 1 '
END
/*========================================================*/
EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt)

/*SELECT (@Qry + @Txt)*/
/*======================================================================*/
/**/
END
